Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the protein object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 552. The moduli space has dimension at most 212 with 3 connected components.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the protein decomposition. By minimality, the element a^552 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 212-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 212.2 (Structural Decomposition)

The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^684(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 212.4 (Invariance Principle)

Statement: For invariance principle pairs (A,B) in protein theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 464 and the invariant I(A,B) = 452 mod 5.
